```
ks = np.arange(-(N-1), N)
starts = np.clip(ks, 0, N-1)
stops = np.clip(N+ks, 1, N)  # N+k, valid k from -N+1..N-1 => -? For k=-N+1, N+k=1, for k=-? stop=1; for k positive up to2N-1 clip N.
diff = np.zeros(N+1)
diff += np.bincount(starts, weights=w, minlength=N+1)
diff -= np.bincount(stops, weights=w, minlength=N+1)
M = np.cumsum(diff)[:-1]
```

Implement `corr_array(h, force_fft=False)`:
```
def corr_array(h):
  n=h.size
  if n <= 700: return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
  L=1
  target=2*n-1
  while L<target: L*=2
  ah=np.fft.rfftfreq? no just rfft(h,L)
  br=(1.0-h)[::-1]
  bc=np.fft.rfft(br,L)
  out=np.fft.irfft(ah*bc, L)
  return out[:2*n-1]
```

Let's design a flexible local optimizer that will likely improve parent:

A. **Smooth SLSQP with full logsumexp and exact gradient**. This is differentiable and can handle continuous h. For each resolution n (450, maybe 675, 900), we run from multiple starts. Objective uses alpha. Since max is the true objective, smooth approximates with high alpha. We can use alpha increasing: e.g., 20,50,100,200,400. SLSQP with equality/bounds. For n=450, maxiter 200? Previous maxiter 70 for alpha120. We can increase if time. But SLSQP may be slow; full gradient via FFT? For n=450, direct gradient maybe easier. We can implement gradient active over top weights but for objective equality, use that? If SLSQP uses smooth_all with full weights, need full gradient (FFT for T, M). Let's implement robustly. Might be overkill but powerful.

However smooth_all objective at high alpha may be very peaked; SLSQP may struggle. We can instead use a **minimax linearization**: At each SLSQP call, use fixed weights w from current best corr (active set top K) in objective `G(h)=Σ w_k c_k`, fixed weights (not recomputed). Then SLSQP minimizes a smooth quadratic (not max) with simple gradient (K active), but as h changes weights fixed may not track max. We can iterate: recompute weights and rerun. This is like alternating projections. Could be efficient and accurate: each subproblem continuous quadratic? G is quadratic, not convex? But SLSQP can. The true max may not decrease monotonically, but we evaluate exact.

Maybe implement **projected gradient with time-varying softmax** simpler and avoid SLSQP overhead.

B. **Active direction with Barzilai-Borwein / backtracking**:
For current h, compute corr, m, weights softmax over top values (or all with alpha). Compute exact gradient g of weighted corr (active K). Remove mean. Then choose step via Armijo for true objective? True objective nonsmooth; use exact C. We can do line search: step = initial; try steps multiplied. Accept if C new < C old - tol or C new close and weighted F decreases. Use multiple directions: -g, -g + random, -g where g computed for top 2,5,10,20. This can converge.

Previous `polish_gradient_active` tried fixed direction -g with step array. We can enhance by using **gradient of max at current max** (weights softmax alpha high) and **adaptive step from previous accepted**. Use a line search with quadratic interpolation.

C. **Pair swap local search** can't be beaten. Use gradient g to pick low/high pools; test many pairs. For each candidate pair, use exact score via FFT/direct. To reduce evaluations, use linear delta to estimate top candidates: ΔF≈δ(g_a - g_b). We can rank pairs by potential improvement. Then evaluate only top P pairs (e.g., 50) per iteration. We can also allow moving one mass to two positions (triple) if pair fails.

D. **Resolution leapfrog**: If best at n=450, upsample to n=900, smooth optimize with SLSQP/gradient, then maybe downsample to n=450? The objective on n=450 could be better because a continuous shape sampled coarser? No, the task objective is on returned n; we can return high n if better. High n may lower max by capturing more degrees but also more lags. Since a continuous function's integral over shifted step functions approximates continuous integral; increasing n should converge to true continuous value. If parent at 450 already near continuous optimum, upsample might not lower, but high-res local can find lower discrete due to grid alignment. We'll try n=450 and n=900. Maybe n=360/375? The discrete optimum may depend on n; choosing a favorable n can lower. Parent uses 450 due previous. We can also try n=300, 225. But returning lower n with objective below target okay. Lower n has fewer degrees but fewer max over lags? The max over lags count 2n-1, but integral scales. Sometimes lower n can yield lower discrete max due to aliasing? The parent 450 maybe near optimum. We can search several n: 150,225,300,450,600,900. For each, upscale best. Time allocation.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300):
 start; deadline = start + budget - 30
 parent validation
 best from parent

Let's implement `project_density` efficient:
```
def project_density(x, target):
 x=np.asarray(x,float).copy(); nan... clip
 n=x.size
 if n==0 return
 s=x.sum()
 if abs(s-target)<1e-12: return x
 # use binary search for shift
 lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
 for _ in range(50):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2
   s2=np.sum(np.clip(x+mid,0,1))
   if s2 < target: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
 h=np.clip(x+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
 res=target-h.sum()
 if abs(res)>1e-10:
   free = np.where((h>0)&(h<1-1e-15))[0]? Use tolerance.
   if free.size: h[free]+=res/free.size; h=np.clip...
   # if still off, adjust one free
```
The binary search sum 50*n per projection. Many projections (line search). Could optimize with fewer iterations 40. We can also use water-filling with sorting? For vector x, projecting onto simplex with bounds [0,1] can be O(n log n) using sort; binary search C maybe faster for n=450? Fine. But many projections (maybe thousands) =>50*450*1000=22.5M ops, okay.

We need preserve exact sum? Evaluation doesn't require exact equality? Task constraint sum(h)*dx=1 exactly? It says sum(h) == n/2 exactly; but evaluator likely just checks value not sum? It may check? Need ensure sum close. Project_density should get within 1e-12. The binary search sum might leave residual 1e-12. We can final adjust with `np.sum` and free idx. But exact floating not possible; within tolerance okay. We can make sum exactly by adding residual to one coordinate if no bound violation. Use `res = target - h.sum()`, find idx not at bound, adjust. Since target is .5*n potentially integer or .5. For n odd target .5; sum of floats maybe. Adjust one h[free]+=res and clip. If infeasible? But there is always free if sum target within [0,n] and not all at bounds. For n even target integer; binary search may yield exact? We'll ensure.

`corr_array` direct:
```
def corr_array(h):
 n=h.size
 if n <= 2200:
   return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
 # FFT fallback
 L=1; target=2*n; while L<target: L<<=1
 ah=np.fft.rfft(h,L)
 bh=np.fft.rfft((1.0-h)[::-1], L)
 out=np.fft.irfft(ah*bh, L)
 return out[:2*n-1]
```
Need ensure `1.0-h` nonneg? h projected [0,1]. Good. `np.fft` allowed.
We can test orientation: conv h and reverse(1-h) full equals correlate? We concluded yes. If not, max same; gradient formula assumes same ordering as np.correlate. Is conv output same ordering? Let's thoroughly test with N=3 formulas:
h=[a,b,c], g=[1-a,1-b,1-c]
conv h * rev(g) = [a(1-c), a(1-b)+b(1-c), a(1-a)+b(1-b)+c(1-c), b(1-a)+c(1-b), c(1-a)]? Let's recompute conv indices:
p0: i0 a g2 = a(1-c)
p1: i0 a g1 + i1 b g2 = a(1-b)+b(1-c)
p2: a g0 + b g1 + c g2 = a(1-a)+b(1-b)+c(1-c)
p3: i1 b g0 + i2 c g1 = b(1-a)+c(1-b)
p4: i2 c g0 = c(1-a)
Numpy correlate(h,g): output[0] for k=-2: i? We resolved output[0]=h0 g2 = a(1-c). output[1] k=-1: h0 g1 + h1 g2 = a(1-b)+b(1-c). output[2] center. output[3] k=1: h1 g0 + h2 g1 = b(1-a)+c(1-b). output[4]=h2 g0=c(1-a). Matches. Good. FFT conv same.

```
def weighted_gradient(h, w):
 n=h.size; center=n-1
 if n==1: return zeros
 K=zeros(2n-1); K[center]=2*w[center]
 if n>1:
   sym = w[:center][::-1] + w[center+1:]
   K[center-1:0:-1] = sym
   K[center+1:] = sym
 M = np.convolve(w, _ones_cache(n), 'full')[center:2*center+1]
 T = np.convolve(h, K, 'full')[center:2*center+1]
 return (2.0/n)*(M-T)
```
Need cache ones for n; can just `np.ones(n)`; but in loop many, allocating each time. Use global dict? Without closure, can use a module-level list `_ONES_CACHE = []`? But top-level variables okay. Simpler: in function, `ones = np.ones(h.size)`. Allocation n=450 *1000 negligible. For n=900 okay.

def active_projected(h, deadline, alphas=(30,80,150), max_total_iter=500):
 n,target...

 iter=0; stall=0

   sub_deadline = min(deadline, time + (deadline-start)*??)
   while time<sub_deadline and iter<max_total_iter:
     corr=corr_array(best); m=corr.max()
     y=alpha*(corr-m); e=exp(clip); w=e/sum
     # top threshold cumulative
     order=np.argsort(-w)

     g = weighted_gradient(best, w2) # includes dx; scale n/2
     g *= n/2.0
     d=-g; d-=mean(d)
     norm=np.linalg.norm(d); if norm<1e-14 break
     d/=norm
     improved=False
     # maybe momentum? maintain v global? local variable in function.
     for step in (2e-4,6e-4,2e-3,6e-3,2e-2,6e-2,0.2,0.5):
       cand=project_density(best+step*d,target); c=score(cand)
       if c < best_c - 1e-13:
          best=cand; best_c=c; improved=True; break
     if improved: stall=0; iter+=1
     else:
       # try orthogonal random shock
       for _ in range(5):
         r=rng.normal(size=n); r-=mean(r); r/=norm
         for step ...
       stall++
       if stall>30: break
   iter+=...
 return best,best_c

def pair_swap_search(h, deadline, n_pos=8, n_neg=8, top_frac=0.1, alpha=50):

   found=False
   # build all pair candidates and estimate potential
   candidates=[]
   for a in low:
     for b in high:
       if a==b: continue
       cap=min(1-best[a], best[b])
       if cap<1e-12: continue
       est=(g[b]-g[a])*cap
       if est>0: candidates.append((est,a,b,cap))
   sort desc; limit 30
   for est,a,b,cap in candidates:
     # try steps fractions [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0] cap
     for frac in (0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0):

       if valid: c=score; if c<best_c improve
   if not found random 20 swaps
   stall...

`make_weights(corr, alpha, cum=0.999)`:
```
def make_weights(corr, alpha, cum=0.999, tol=1e-8):
 z=corr.size
 if z==0: return np.zeros(0)
 m=corr.max()
 y=alpha*(corr-m); y=np.clip(y,-745,0)
 e=np.exp(y); s=e.sum()
 if s<=0 or not finite:
   idx=np.argmax(corr); w=zeros; w[idx]=1; return w
 w=e/s
 # cumulative top
 order=np.argsort(-w)
 cumw=np.cumsum(w[order])
 k=int(np.searchsorted(cumw, cum))+1
 if k < 1: k=1
 if k < z and w[order[k]] < 1e-8: ...
 # zero below order[k] maybe
 w2=w.copy()
 w2[order[k:]] = 0.0
 s2=w2.sum()
 if s2>0: w2/=s2
 else: w=w
 return w2
```
This threshold with cum 0.999 includes enough. For gradient of true smooth max, we should not truncate; but for active direction okay. For SLSQP full smooth, we should use full weights (not truncated) in objective/jac to match objective. So SLSQP's jac must recompute full w (not truncated). Active projected can use truncated.

`weighted_gradient_fast(h,w)`:
- n, center. Build K as before.
- L = next power of two >= 3*n? For M = conv w*ones: can use discrete difference O(n) to avoid FFT. For T = conv h*K: use FFT. M can be computed via diff/bincount O(n) faster; simpler use `np.convolve` maybe fine. For high n, implement `M_via_bins(w,n)`:
```
def linear_conv_rect(w,n):
  # w length 2n-1, return M length n = conv(w, ones(n))[n-1:2n-1]
  # Use bincount difference:
  ks = np.arange(-(n-1), n)
  starts = np.clip(ks, 0, n-1)
  stops = np.clip(n+ks, 1, n)
  diff = np.zeros(n+1)
  diff += np.bincount(starts, weights=w, minlength=n+1)
  diff -= np.bincount(stops, weights=w, minlength=n+1)
  np.cumsum(diff, out=diff)
  return diff[:n]
```
This is O(n) vectorized, good. Use for all n? For small maybe bincount overhead but fine. Let's use this for M to avoid conv. Need ensure `starts` int, `stops` int.

`conv_center(h,K,n)`:
```
center=n-1
if n <= 400:
  out=np.convolve(h,K,'full'); return out[center:2*center+1]
else:
  need=3*n-2; L=1
  while L<need: L<<=1
  H=np.fft.rfft(h,L); KK=np.fft.rfft(K,L)

```

`diff_conv_rect(w,n)`:
```
if n <= 400:
  out=np.convolve(w, np.ones(n), 'full'); return out[n-1:2*n-1]
ks=np.arange(-(n-1), n, dtype=int)
starts=np.clip(ks,0,n-1)
stops=np.clip(n+ks,1,n)
diff=np.zeros(n+1)
diff += np.bincount(starts, weights=w, minlength=n+1)
diff -= np.bincount(stops, weights=w, minlength=n+1)
np.cumsum(diff, out=diff)
return diff[:n]
```
Test with k0: w center 1, ls length 2n-1. starts: k=-n+1..n-1 => after clip many? For k=0 start0? Wait k=0 contributes interval j∈[0,n-1], start0, stopn. For centers? Our formula from earlier: for lag k, interval start=max(0,k), stop=min(n,N+k). For k=0 start0 stopn. Good. For k=+d start=d stop n; for k=-d start0 stop n-d. Yes. Weighted diff cum gives M. Good.

`weighted_gradient` with M/T:
```
def weighted_gradient(h,w):
 n=h.size; center=n-1
 s=w.sum()
 if s<=0: return zeros
 if abs(s-1)>1e-12: w=w/s
 K=np.zeros(2*n-1)
 w0=w[center]
 K[center]=2*w0
 if n>1:
   sym = w[:center][::-1] + w[center+1:]
   K[center-1:0:-1] = sym
   K[center+1:] = sym
 M = rect_conv(w,n)
 T = conv_center(h,K,n)
 return (2.0/n) * (M - T)
```
Test with all lags? Good.

Project:
```
def project_density(x,target):
   x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
   if x.size==0: return x
   x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
   x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
   target=float(target)
   if target<0: target=0
   if target>x.size: target=x.size
   s=float(np.sum(x))
   if abs(s-target)<=1e-13: return x
   lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
   # If s>target, need decrease; if s< increase; binary shift within [-max, max]. Bound [-1,1] enough because x in [0,1]
   for _ in range(50):
      mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
      s2=float(np.sum(np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0)))
      if s2 < target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   h=np.clip(x+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
   res=target-float(np.sum(h))
   if abs(res)>1e-12:
      free=np.where((h>1e-15)&(h<1.0-1e-15))[0]
      if free.size:
         h[free]+=res/free.size
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
         res2=target-float(np.sum(h))
         if abs(res2)>1e-13:
            # adjust first free with max room
            for idx in free:
               room = (1.0 if res2>0 else h[idx]) - h[idx]? easier
               if res2>0 and h[idx]+res2 <=1+1e-12:
                  h[idx]+=res2; break
               if res2<0 and h[idx]+res2 >=-1e-12:
                  h[idx]+=res2; break
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
```
Need ensure if free size zero but res not zero? Could happen all at bounds; target in [0,n], then residual should zero within tolerance. If not, adjust one bound? But infeasible if all 0 or1 and target mismatch. Could adjust arbitrary: if res>0 add to any h<1 else res<0 from any h>0. If none, clip. Good.

def make_fourier_h(n, coeffs):
 x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
 h=np.full(n,0.5)
 for i in range(len(coeffs)//2):
   m=i+1; a=coeffs[2*i]; b=coeffs[2*i+1]
   h += a*np.cos(m*math.pi*x) + b*np.sin(m*math.pi*x)

```
def corr_array(h):
 n=h.size
 if n <= 1500:
   return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
 L=1; need=2*n-1
 while L<need: L <<=1
 ah=np.fft.rfft(h,L)
 bh=np.fft.rfft((1.0-h)[::-1],L)
 out=np.fft.irfft(ah*bh,L)
 return out[:need]
```
For n=1500, direct 1.5M? sum positive n(n-1)/2=1.125M, total2.25M, okay.

For `conv_center` FFT if n>350. Need `K` and `h` maybe length. Use:
```
def conv_center(h,K,n):
 center=n-1
 if n <= 350:
   out=np.convolve(h,K,'full'); return out[center:2*center+1]
 need=3*n-2
 L=1
 while L<need: L<<=1
 H=np.fft.rfft(h,L); KK=np.fft.rfft(K,L)
 out=np.fft.irfft(H*KK,L)
 return out[center:2*center+1]
```
Check L for n=351: need1051, L2048; okay.

`rect_conv` for M use bincount for n > 200 maybe; but for all:
```
def rect_conv(w,n):
 if n <= 200:
   out=np.convolve(w,np.ones(n),'full'); return out[n-1:2*n-1]
 ks=np.arange(-(n-1),n,dtype=int)
 starts=np.clip(ks,0,n-1)
 stops=np.clip(n+ks,1,n)
 diff=np.zeros(n+1)
 diff += np.bincount(starts, weights=w, minlength=n+1)
 diff -= np.bincount(stops, weights=w, minlength=n+1)
 np.cumsum(diff, out=diff)
 return diff[:n]
```
This is O(n) and probably fast even small. Use always to avoid conv. For n=450, bincount with arrays length 899 fine. Let's use always. Need dtype int for starts/stops. Good.

Implementation active_projected in detail:
```
def active_projected(h, deadline, alphas=(20,60,150), max_iter=300, cum=0.999, rng=None):
 if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(2024)
 n=h.size; target=0.5*n
 best=project_density(h,target); best_c=c5_value(best)
 if n<2 or time.perf_counter()>=deadline: return best,best_c
 v=np.zeros(n)
 total=0
 for alpha in alphas:
   v.fill(0)
   stall=0
   while time.perf_counter()<deadline and total<max_iter:
     corr=corr_array(best); m=float(corr.max())
     w=make_weights(corr,alpha,cum)
     g=weighted_gradient(best,w)
     g = g * (float(n)*0.5)
     d=-g; d-=float(np.mean(d))
     norm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
     dirs=[]
     if norm>1e-14:
       dirs.append(d/norm)
       v=0.6*v + 0.4*d/norm
       v-=float(np.mean(v)); nvm=float(np.linalg.norm(v))
       if nvm>1e-12: dirs.append(v/nvm)
     improved=False
     for dr in dirs:
       for step in (5e-5,1.5e-4,5e-4,1.5e-3,5e-3,1.5e-2,5e-2,0.15,0.4):
         cand=project_density(best+step*dr,target)
         c=c5_value(cand)
         if c < best_c - 1e-12:
           best=cand; best_c=c; improved=True; break
       if improved: break
     if improved:
       total+=1; stall=0
     else:
       found=False
       for _ in range(6):
         r=rng.standard_normal(n); r-=float(np.mean(r)); nr=float(np.linalg.norm(r))
         if nr<1e-12: continue
         r/=nr
         for step in (1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2):
           cand=project_density(best+step*r,target)
           c=c5_value(cand)
           if c < best_c - 1e-12:
             best=cand; best_c=c; found=True; total+=1; break
         if found: break
       if not found:
         stall+=1
         if stall>25: break
   if best_c <= 0.350: break
 return best,best_c
```
Maybe step 0.4 with unit direction can change L2 0.4; projection may drastically alter. Acceptance ensures if improves. Good.
But evaluating 9 steps *2 dirs *300 =5400 scores; n450 0.2ms ->1s. fine.

def pair_swap_search(h, deadline, n_pos=10, n_neg=10, alpha=60, max_iter=1500, cum=0.999, rng=None):
 if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(98765)
 n=h.size; target=0.5*n
 best=project; best_c=score
 if n<2: return
 stall=0
 for it in range(max_iter):
   if time>=deadline: break
   corr=corr_array(best); 
   w=make_weights(corr,alpha,cum)
   g=weighted_gradient(best,w)*(n/2)
   low=np.argsort(g)[:n_neg]
   high=np.argsort(-g)[:n_pos]
   cands=[]
   for ai in low:
     a=int(ai); gab? 
     for bi in high:
       b=int(bi)
       if a==b: continue
       if g[b] <= g[a]: continue
       cap=min(1-best[a], best[b])
       if cap<1e-12: continue
       cands.append(((g[b]-g[a])*cap, a,b,cap))
   cands.sort(reverse=True)
   found=False
   for item in cands[:40]:
     a,b,cap=item[1],item[2],item[3]
     for frac in (0.15,0.4,0.7,1.0):
       d=frac*cap
       nb=[best? can't list assign]
       cand=best.copy(); cand[a]+=d; cand[b]-=d
       if cand[a] < -1e-12 or cand[a] > 1+1e-12 or cand[b] < -1e-12 or cand[b] > 1+1e-12: continue
       c=c5_value(cand)
       if c < best_c - 1e-12:
         best=cand; best_c=c; found=True; break
     if found: break
   if not found:
      # random pairs
      for _ in range(12):
         a=int(rng.integers(n)); b=int(rng.integers(n))
         if a==b: continue
         cap=min(1-best[a], best[b])
         if cap<1e-12: continue
         d=0.25*cap
         cand=best.copy(); cand[a]+=d; cand[b]-=d
         if valid: c=score; if c<best_c-1e-12: best=...
      if not found: stall+=1 else stall=0
      if stall>30: break
   else: stall=0
 return best, c5_value(best)
```

    if abs(res) <= 1e-13: break
    if res > 0:
        free = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-14)[0]
    else:
        free = np.where(h > 1e-14)[0]
    if free.size == 0: break
    h[free] += res / free.size
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
res = target - float(np.sum(h))
if abs(res)>1e-14:
    if res>0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
       if h[idx]+res <= 1.0+1e-12:
          h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx]+res,0.0,1.0))
    else:

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_ALP = 1.0
_NS = 0
_CON_TARGET = 0.0
_MAX_OPT_N = 1000


def corr_array(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n <= 1500:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    ah = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    bh = np.fft.rfft((1.0 - h)[::-1], L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(ah * bh, L)
    return out[:need]


def c5_value(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    corr = corr_array(h)
    if corr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return m * (2.0 / float(n))


def project_density(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    target = float(target)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, -1.0, 1.0)
    if target < 0.0:
        target = 0.0
    if target > float(n):
        target = float(n)

    if float(x.min()) >= -1e-15 and float(x.max()) <= 1.0 + 1e-15:
        if abs(float(np.sum(x)) - target) <= 1e-13:
            return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

    xc = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(np.sum(xc)) - target) <= 1e-13:
        return xc

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(48):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(5):
        res = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(res) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if res > 0.0:
            free = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-14)[0]
        else:
            free = np.where(h > 1e-14)[0]
        if free.size == 0:
            break
        h[free] += res / float(free.size)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    res = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(res) > 1e-14:
        if res > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            if h[idx] + res <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
                h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + res, 0.0, 1.0))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            if h[idx] + res >= -1e-12:
                h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + res, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h


def upsample_h(parent, n):
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
    n0 = p.size
    if n0 <= 0:
        return project_density(np.full(max(1, int(n)), 0.5), 0.5 * max(1, int(n)))
    n = int(n)
    if n0 == n:
        return p.copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = np.interp(x1, x0, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * float(n))


def make_default_start(n):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5
    h += 0.25 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    h += 0.12 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    h -= 0.08 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
    h += 0.05 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * float(n))


def sum_constraint(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _CON_TARGET)


def sum_constraint_jac(x):
    return np.ones(np.asarray(x, dtype=float).shape, dtype=float)


def rect_conv(w, n):
    n = int(n)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    if n <= 1:
        return w[:1].copy()
    ks = np.arange(-(n - 1), n, dtype=int)
    starts = np.clip(ks, 0, n - 1)
    stops = np.clip(n + ks, 1, n)
    diff = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    diff += np.bincount(starts, weights=w, minlength=n + 1)
    diff -= np.bincount(stops, weights=w, minlength=n + 1)
    np.cumsum(diff, out=diff)
    return diff[:n]


def conv_center(h, k, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    k = np.asarray(k, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    if n <= 350:
        out = np.convolve(h, k, mode="full")
        return out[center:2 * center + 1]
    need = 3 * n - 2
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    K = np.fft.rfft(k, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(H * K, L)
    return out[center:2 * center + 1]


def weighted_gradient(h, w):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-14:
        w = w / s
    center = n - 1
    K = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    w0 = float(w[center])
    K[center] = 2.0 * w0
    if n > 1:
        sym = w[:center][::-1] + w[center + 1:]
        K[center - 1:0:-1] = sym
        K[center + 1:] = sym
    M = rect_conv(w, n)
    T = conv_center(h, K, n)
    g = (2.0 / float(n)) * (M - T)
    return np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def make_weights(corr, alpha, cum=0.999):
    corr = np.asarray(corr, dtype=float)
    corr = np.nan_to_num(corr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = corr.size
    if z == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    y = float(alpha) * (corr - m)
    y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(z, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(corr))] = 1.0
        return w
    w = e / s
    order = np.argsort(-w)
    cumw = np.cumsum(w[order])
    k = int(np.searchsorted(cumw, float(cum))) + 1
    if k < 1:
        k = 1
    if k > z:
        k = z
    if k < z:
        w2 = w.copy()
        w2[order[k:]] = 0.0
        s2 = float(np.sum(w2))
        if s2 > 1e-15:
            return w2 / s2
    return w


def smooth_full_obj(x):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = int(_NS)
    if n <= 1 or h.size != n:
        return 1e100
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    corr = corr_array(h)
    if corr.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100
    y = _ALP * (corr - m)
    y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e100
    return float((2.0 / float(n)) * (m + float(np.log(s)) / float(_ALP)))


def smooth_full_jac(x):
    n = int(_NS)
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if n <= 1 or h.size != n:
        return np.zeros(max(1, n), dtype=float)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    corr = corr_array(h)
    if corr.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    y = _ALP * (corr - m)
    y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w = e / s
    return weighted_gradient(h, w)


def active_projected(h, deadline, alphas=(20.0, 60.0, 150.0), max_iter=300, cum=0.999, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240521)
    n = int(h.size)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    best = project_density(h, target)
    best_c = c5_value(best)
    if n < 2 or time.perf_counter() >= deadline:
        return best, best_c

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    total = 0
    steps = (5e-05, 1.5e-04, 5e-04, 1.5e-03, 5e-03, 1.5e-02, 5e-02, 0.15, 0.4)

    for alpha in alphas:
        v.fill(0.0)
        stall = 0
        while time.perf_counter() < deadline and total < max_iter:
            corr = corr_array(best)
            corr = np.nan_to_num(corr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            if corr.size == 0:
                break
            w = make_weights(corr, float(alpha), cum)
            g = weighted_gradient(best, w)
            g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            g = g * (0.5 * float(n))
            d = -g
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            d = np.nan_to_num(d, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            nd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            dirs = []
            if nd > 1e-14:
                dirs.append(d / nd)
                v = 0.6 * v + 0.4 * (d / nd)
                v -= float(np.mean(v))
                nv = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
                if nv > 1e-12:
                    dirs.append(v / nv)

            improved = False
            for dr in dirs:
                for step in steps:
                    cand = project_density(best + step * dr, target)
                    c = c5_value(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best = cand
                        best_c = c
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

            if improved:
                total += 1
                stall = 0
            else:
                found = False
                for _ in range(6):
                    r = rng.standard_normal(n)
                    r -= float(np.mean(r))
                    nr = float(np.linalg.norm(r))
                    if nr < 1e-12:
                        continue
                    r = r / nr
                    for step in (1e-03, 3e-03, 1e-02, 3e-02):
                        cand = project_density(best + step * r, target)
                        c = c5_value(cand)
                        if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-12:
                            best = cand
                            best_c = c
                            found = True
                            total += 1
                            break
                    if found:
                        break
                if not found:
                    stall += 1
                    if stall > 25:
                        break

    best = project_density(best, target)
    return best, c5_value(best)


def pair_swap_search(h, deadline, n_pos=10, n_neg=10, alpha=60.0, cum=0.999, max_iter=1500, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(170717)
    n = int(h.size)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    best = project_density(h, target)
    best_c = c5_value(best)
    if n < 2:
        return best, best_c

    stall = 0
    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.perf_counter() >= deadline:
            break
        corr = corr_array(best)
        corr = np.nan_to_num(corr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        if corr.size == 0:
            break
        w = make_weights(corr, float(alpha), cum)
        g = weighted_gradient(best, w)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g = g * (0.5 * float(n))

        low = np.argsort(g)[:max(1, int(n_neg))]
        high = np.argsort(-g)[:max(1, int(n_pos))]
        cands = []
        for ai in low:
            a = int(ai)
            for bi in high:
                b = int(bi)
                if a == b:
                    continue
                if g[b] <= g[a]:
                    continue
                cap = min(1.0 - float(best[a]), float(best[b]))
                if cap < 1e-12:
                    continue
                est = (float(g[b]) - float(g[a])) * float(cap)
                if np.isfinite(est) and est > 0.0:
                    cands.append((est, a, b, cap))
        cands.sort(reverse=True)

        found = False
        for item in cands[:40]:
            a = int(item[1])
            b = int(item[2])
            cap = float(item[3])
            for frac in (0.15, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0):
                d = frac * cap
                if d <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                va = float(best[a]) + d
                vb = float(best[b]) - d
                if va < -1e-12 or va > 1.0 + 1e-12 or vb < -1e-12 or vb > 1.0 + 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand = best.copy()
                cand[a] = va
                cand[b] = vb
                c = c5_value(cand)
                if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best = cand
                    best_c = c
                    found = True
                    break
            if found:
                break

        if not found:
            for _r in range(12):
                a = int(rng.integers(n))
                b = int(rng.integers(n))
                if a == b:
                    continue
                cap = min(1.0 - float(best[a]), float(best[b]))
                if cap < 1e-12:
                    continue
                d = 0.25 * cap
                if d <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                va = float(best[a]) + d
                vb = float(best[b]) - d
                if va < -1e-12 or va > 1.0 + 1e-12 or vb < -1e-12 or vb > 1.0 + 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand = best.copy()
                cand[a] = va
                cand[b] = vb
                c = c5_value(cand)
                if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best = cand
                    best_c = c
                    found = True
                    break

        if found:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 30:
                break

    best = project_density(best, target)
    return best, c5_value(best)


def make_fourier_h(n, coeffs):
    n = int(n)
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=float)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    m = 0
    i = 0
    while i + 1 < coeffs.size and m < max(1, n // 2):
        m += 1
        a = float(coeffs[i])
        b = float(coeffs[i + 1])
        theta = float(m) * np.pi * x
        h += a * np.cos(theta)
        h += b * np.sin(theta)
        i += 2
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * float(n))


def make_fixed_starts(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    out = []

    pats = (
        (0.25, 0.00, 0.12, 0.00, -0.08, 0.00, 0.05, 0.00),
        (0.20, 0.10, 0.15, -0.05, 0.08, 0.10, -0.03, 0.05),
        (0.18, 0.00, 0.20, 0.00, 0.12, 0.00, -0.05, 0.00),
        (0.30, -0.05, 0.10, 0.08, -0.05, -0.05, 0.05, 0.10),
        (0.15, 0.15, -0.10, 0.10, 0.10, -0.10, 0.05, 0.05),
    )
    for p in pats:
        out.append(make_fourier_h(n, np.array(p, dtype=float)))

    reps = max(1, n // 6)
    for pat in ((0.85, 0.45, 0.15, 0.85, 0.45, 0.15),
                (0.90, 0.20, 0.70, 0.20, 0.90, 0.20),
                (0.80, 0.30, 0.50, 0.30, 0.80, 0.50)):
        hh = []
        for v in pat:
            for _j in range(reps):
                hh.append(float(v))
        hh = np.array(hh[:n], dtype=float)
        if hh.size == n:
            out.append(project_density(hh, 0.5 * float(n)))

    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.2831853))
    ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.2831853))
    hh = 0.5 + 0.28 * np.sin(np.pi * x + ph1) + 0.16 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + ph2)
    hh += 0.08 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)
    out.append(project_density(hh, 0.5 * float(n)))

    for hh in out:
        hh = project_density(hh, 0.5 * float(n))
        hh = np.nan_to_num(hh, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        out[-1] = hh
    return out


def polish_candidate(h, deadline, use_slsqp=True, quick=False):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n < 2:
        h = make_default_start(90)
        n = int(h.size)
    if n > _MAX_OPT_N:
        h = upsample_h(h, _MAX_OPT_N)
        n = int(h.size)

    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    best = project_density(h, target)
    best_c = c5_value(best)

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if use_slsqp and rem > 15.0 and n <= 1000:
        if quick:
            alphas = (15.0, 50.0, 120.0)
            maxits = (35, 35, 35)
        else:
            if n <= 500:
                alphas = (15.0, 40.0, 100.0, 220.0)
                maxits = (60, 60, 60, 50)
            else:
                alphas = (15.0, 50.0, 120.0)
                maxits = (55, 50, 45)

        _CON_TARGET = target
        bounds = []
        for _i in range(n):
            bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))
        constraints = ({"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint, "jac": sum_constraint_jac},)

        per = max(2.0, (deadline - time.perf_counter()) * 0.5 / float(len(alphas)))
        for idx, alpha in enumerate(alphas):
            if time.perf_counter() >= deadline - 6.0:
                break
            _NS = n
            _ALP = float(alpha)
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    smooth_full_obj,
                    best.copy(),
                    method="SLSQP",
                    jac=smooth_full_jac,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=constraints,
                    options={"maxiter": int(maxits[idx]), "ftol": 1e-11, "disp": False},
                )
                if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                    cand = project_density(res.x, target)
                    c = c5_value(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c:
                        best = cand
                        best_c = c
            except Exception:
                pass
        best = project_density(best, target)
        best_c = c5_value(best)

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 10.0:
        if quick:
            sub = rem * 0.25
            mit = 130
        else:
            sub = rem * 0.35
            mit = 350 if n <= 600 else 220
        h2, c2 = active_projected(
            best,
            time.perf_counter() + sub,
            alphas=(15.0, 50.0, 150.0),
            max_iter=int(mit),
            cum=0.999,
        )
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
            best = h2
            best_c = c2

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 8.0:
        if quick:
            sub = rem * 0.50
            mit = 450
        else:
            sub = rem * 0.55
            mit = 1300 if n <= 600 else 800
        h3, c3 = pair_swap_search(
            best,
            time.perf_counter() + sub,
            n_pos=9 if quick else 11,
            n_neg=9 if quick else 11,
            alpha=70.0,
            cum=0.999,
            max_iter=int(mit),
        )
        if np.isfinite(c3) and c3 < best_c:
            best = h3
            best_c = c3

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 6.0:
        h4, c4 = active_projected(
            best,
            time.perf_counter() + rem * 0.85,
            alphas=(50.0, 160.0),
            max_iter=130 if quick else 260,
            cum=0.999,
        )
        if np.isfinite(c4) and c4 < best_c:
            best = h4
            best_c = c4

    best = project_density(best, target)
    return best, c5_value(best)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    budget = max(20.0, budget)
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(10.0, budget - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        raw = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if raw.ndim == 1 and raw.size >= 10:
            raw = np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
            raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1.0)
            p = project_density(raw, 0.5 * float(raw.size))
            cp = c5_value(p)
            if np.isfinite(cp):
                best_h = p
                best_c = cp
                best_n = int(p.size)
    except Exception:
        best_h = None

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = make_default_start(450)
        best_c = c5_value(best_h)
        best_n = int(best_h.size)

    rem = hard_deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 20.0:
        a_time = min(520.0, max(60.0, 0.42 * rem))
        dl_a = time.perf_counter() + a_time
        try:
            hA, cA = polish_candidate(best_h, dl_a, use_slsqp=True, quick=False)
            if np.isfinite(cA) and cA < best_c:
                best_h = hA
                best_c = cA
                best_n = int(best_h.size)
        except Exception:
            pass

    rem = hard_deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 160.0 and best_n <= 1000:
        n2 = int(min(_MAX_OPT_N, max(best_n + 1, 2 * best_n)))
        if n2 > best_n:
            hB = upsample_h(best_h, n2)
            cB0 = c5_value(hB)
            if np.isfinite(cB0) and cB0 < best_c + 0.08:
                b_time = min(450.0, max(60.0, 0.38 * rem))
                dl_b = time.perf_counter() + b_time
                try:
                    hB2, cB2 = polish_candidate(hB, dl_b, use_slsqp=True, quick=False)
                    if np.isfinite(cB2) and cB2 < best_c:
                        best_h = hB2
                        best_c = cB2
                        best_n = int(best_h.size)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    rem = hard_deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 140.0:
        nstart = int(min(600, max(300, best_n if best_n > 0 else 450)))
        if nstart < 300:
            nstart = 300
        cands = []
        if best_n != nstart or best_n <= 0:
            cands.append(upsample_h(best_h, nstart))
        cands.extend(make_fixed_starts(nstart, rng))

        vals = []
        hs = []
        for hh in cands:
            try:
                hh = project_density(hh, 0.5 * float(hh.size))
                cc = c5_value(hh)
                if np.isfinite(cc):
                    vals.append(cc)
                    hs.append(hh)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if vals:
            order = np.argsort(np.asarray(vals, dtype=float))
            tries = 0
            for oi in order[:5]:
                if time.perf_counter() >= hard_deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                if vals[oi] > best_c + 0.07 and best_c < 0.39:
                    break
                if tries >= 3:
                    break
                ctime = min(110.0, max(30.0, (hard_deadline - time.perf_counter()) / float(max(1, 3 - tries))))
                try:
                    hopt, copt = polish_candidate(hs[int(oi)], time.perf_counter() + ctime, use_slsqp=True, quick=True)
                    if np.isfinite(copt) and copt < best_c:
                        best_h = hopt
                        best_c = copt
                        best_n = int(best_h.size)
                    tries += 1
                except Exception:
                    pass

    rem = hard_deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if best_h is not None and rem > 12.0:
        use_final_slsqp = rem > 70.0 and int(best_h.size) <= 1000
        try:
            hF, cF = polish_candidate(
                best_h,
                hard_deadline - 2.0,
                use_slsqp=use_final_slsqp,
                quick=False,
            )
            if np.isfinite(cF) and cF < best_c:
                best_h = hF
                best_c = cF
                best_n = int(best_h.size)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = make_default_start(180)
        best_n = int(best_h.size)

    final_h = project_density(best_h, 0.5 * float(best_h.size))
    final_c = c5_value(final_h)
    if not np.isfinite(final_c):
        final_h = make_default_start(180)
        final_c = c5_value(final_h)
        final_h = project_density(final_h, 0.5 * float(final_h.size))
        final_n = int(final_h.size)
    else:
        final_n = int(final_h.size)

    return final_h, float(final_c), int(final_n)
```
